Control totals

Per-job control figures published by evaluation operations. They are anchors for checking a reconstruction; they are not results.

Per-source notes

eval_store.db:

  • jobs_ledger counts in eval_store.db include rows later reversed or voided; a ledger count above the live row count is expected.

eval_store_mirror.db:

  • jobs_ledger counts in eval_store_mirror.db include rows later reversed or voided; a ledger count above the live row count is expected.
